 \ ]_{\overline{{O}_{T+s}^s}}+\lvert\!\lVert  \mathbf{R}^{+}_{s}\textcolor{red}{\mathcal{U}} \rVert\!\rvert_{\gamma_1,0,O_{T+s}^s}^{(s)}\big].
			\end{split}

		\textbf{Step 3.}\label{Step33}
		Let us now estimate
		\begin{align*}
			\sup_{\substack{z,w \in O_{T+s}^{s},z\neq w\\ d(z,w)\leq \Vert z,w \Vert_{P_{s}}}}\sum_{i=1}^{d}
			\frac{
				\Vert z,w \Vert_{P_{s}}^{\gamma_1}
				\, \big\Vert \mathrm{Pr}_{\large{\text{\color{red}{$X^i$}}}}\big[
				\big(F(\textcolor{red}{\mathcal{U}})-F(\textcolor{red}{\widetilde{\mathcal{U}}})\big)(z)
				-\Gamma_{z,w}\big(\big(F(\textcolor{red}{\mathcal{U}})-F(\textcolor{red}{\widetilde{\mathcal{U}}})\big)(w)\big)
				\big]\big\Vert
			}{
				d(z,w)^{\gamma_1-1}
			}.
		\end{align*}
		Analogous to \eqref{Aza41}, we have
		\begin{align*}
			&	\mathrm{Pr}_{\large{\text{\color{red}{$X^i$}}}}\big[
			F(\textcolor{red}{\mathcal{U}})(z)-\Gamma_{z,w}(
			F(\textcolor{red}{\mathcal{U}})(w)
			)\big]\\&= \mathcal{U}^{\large{\text{\color{red}{$X^i$}}}}(z)\big[F^\prime(\mathcal{U}^{\large{\text{\color{red}{$1$}}}}(z))-F^\prime(\mathcal{U}^{\large{\text{\color{red}{$1$}}}}(w))\big]+F^\prime(\mathcal{U}^{\large{\text{\color{red}{$1$}}}}(w))\big[\mathcal{U}^{\large{\text{\color{red}{$X^i$}}}}(z)-\mathcal{U}^{\large{\text{\color{red}{$X^i$}}}}(w)\big]\\&
			=\mathcal{U}^{\large{\text{\color{red}{$X^i$}}}}(z)	\int_{0}^{1} F^{\prime\prime}\big(
			\alpha \mathcal{U}^{\large{\text{\color{red}{$1$}}}}(z)
			+ (1-\alpha)\mathcal{U}^{\large{\text{\color{red}{$1$}}}}(w)\big)\mathrm{Pr}_{\large{\text{\color{red}{$1$}}}}\big[
			\textcolor{red}{\mathcal{U}}(z)-\Gamma_{z,w}\big(
			\textcolor{red}{\mathcal{U}}(w)
			\big)\big]\mathrm{d}\alpha \\&+\mathcal{U}^{\large{\text{\color{red}{$X^i$}}}}(z)	\int_{0}^{1} F^{\prime\prime}\big(
			\alpha \mathcal{U}^{\large{\text{\color{red}{$1$}}}}(z)
			+ (1-\alpha)\mathcal{U}^{\large{\text{\color{red}{$1$}}}}(w)\big)[ \eta_1(z,w)
			+\eta_2(z,w)\big]\mathrm{d}\alpha+F^\prime(\mathcal{U}^{\large{\text{\color{red}{$1$}}}}(w))\underbrace{\big[\mathcal{U}^{\large{\text{\color{red}{$X^i$}}}}(z)-\mathcal{U}^{\large{\text{\color{red}{$X^i$}}}}(w)\big]}_{\mathrm{Pr}_{\large{\text{\color{red}{$X^i$}}}}\big[
				\textcolor{red}{\mathcal{U}}(z)-\Gamma_{z,w}(
				\textcolor{red}{\mathcal{U}}(w)
				)\big]} .
		\end{align*}
		Therefore, for each $1 \leq i \leq d$, it holds that
		
		\begin{align}\label{NNMass}
			\begin{split}
				&\big\Vert 	\mathrm{Pr}_{\large{\text{\color{red}{$X^i$}}}}\big[
				\big(F(\textcolor{red}{\mathcal{U}})-F(\textcolor{red}{\widetilde{\mathcal{U}}})\big)(z)-\Gamma_{z,w}\big(\big(F(\textcolor{red}{\mathcal{U}})-F(\textcolor{red}{\widetilde{\mathcal{U}}})\big)(w)\big)
				\big]\big\Vert\\ \lesssim
				&\Vert  \mathcal{U}^{\large{\text{\color{red}{$X^i$}}}}(z)-\widetilde{\mathcal{U}}^{\large{\text{\color{red}{$X^i$}}}}(z)\Vert\bigg[\big\Vert\mathrm{Pr}_{\large{\text{\color{red}{$1$}}}}\big[
				\textcolor{red}{\mathcal{U}}(z)-\Gamma_{z,w}\left(
				\textcolor{red}{\mathcal{U}}(w)
				\right)\big]\big\Vert+\Vert\eta_1(z,w)\Vert+\Vert\eta_2(z,w)\Vert\bigg]\\&+\Vert\widetilde{\mathcal{U}}^{\large{\text{\color{red}{$X^i$}}}}(z)\Vert
				M_{\nu}\Big(
				\Vert \mathcal{U}^{\large{\text{\color{red}{$1$}}}}(z)-\widetilde{\mathcal{U}}^{\large{\text{\color{red}{$1$}}}}(z)\Vert
				+
				\Vert \mathcal{U}^{\large{\text{\color{red}{$1$}}}}(w)-\widetilde{\mathcal{U}}^{\large{\text{\color{red}{$1$}}}}(w)\Vert
				\Big)
				\\
				&\times

				+\Vert\eta_1(z,w)\Vert

				\bigg]\\&+\Vert\widetilde{\mathcal{U}}^{\large{\text{\color{red}{$X^i$}}}}(z)\Vert \bigg[\big\Vert\mathrm{Pr}_{\large{\text{\color{red}{$1$}}}}\big[

		For the second line of \eqref{NNMass}, one can see that

			&z,w \in O_{T+s}^{s}, \quad
			d(z,w)\leq \Vert z,w \Vert_{P_{s}}

			\bigg[

\ ]_{\overline{{O}_{T+s}^s}}\,

\ ]_{\overline{{O}_{T+s}^s}}

			+

			\lvert\!\lVert \mathbf{R}^{+}_{s}\textcolor{red}{\mathcal{U}} \rVert\!\rvert_{\gamma_1,0,O_{T+s}^s}^{(s)}.

		The same argument applies to the other terms on the right-hand side of \eqref{NNMass}.
		Therefore, we conclude that

 \ ]_{\overline{{O}_{T+s}^s}}+\lvert\!\lVert  \mathbf{R}^{+}_{s}\textcolor{red}{{\mathcal{U}}} \rVert\!\rvert_{\gamma_1,0,O_{T+s}^s}^{(s)}\big)\big(1+\lvert\!\lVert  \mathbf{R}^{+}_{s}\textcolor{red}{\widetilde{\mathcal{U}}} \rVert\!\rvert_{\gamma_1,0,O_{T+s}^s}^{(s)}\big).

		\textbf{Step 4.}\label{Step44} Now we can finish the proof. Note that, from \eqref{Hnasssss} and the assumptions on $F$ and $\Sigma$, for each
		$a\in \{\large{\text{\color{red}{$1$}}}, 
	\mathrel{ 
		\vcenter{

	} 
,\large{\text{\color{red}{$X^i$}}}: i=1,\dots,d\}$, we have
		\begin{align}\label{Z44}
			\sup_{z\in O_{T+s}^{s}}
			\Vert z\Vert_{P_s}^{|a|_{h}}
			\big\Vert\mathrm{Pr}_{a}\big[
			\big(F(\textcolor{red}{\mathcal{U}})-F(\textcolor{red}{\widetilde{\mathcal{U}}})\big)(z)
			\big]\big\Vert
			\lesssim
			\Big(
			1+\lvert\!\lVert \mathbf{R}^{+}_{s}\textcolor{red}{{\mathcal{U}}} \rVert\!\rvert_{\gamma_1,0,O_{T+s}^s}^{(s)}
			\Big)
			\lvert\!\lVert \mathbf{R}^{+}_{s}\textcolor{red}{\mathcal{U}}-\mathbf{R}^{+}_{s}\textcolor{red}{{\mathcal{U}}} \rVert\!\rvert_{\gamma_1,0,O_{T+s}^s}^{(s)}.
		\end{align}
		By \eqref{Z11}, \eqref{Z22}, \eqref{Z33}, and \eqref{Z44}, we deduce that
		\begin{align*}
			&\lvert\!\lVert \mathbf{R}^{+}_{s}F\big(\textcolor{red}{\mathcal{U}}\big) - \mathbf{R}^{+}_{s}F\big(\textcolor{red}{\widetilde{\mathcal{U}}}\big) \rVert\!\rvert_{\gamma_1,0,O_{T+s}^s}^{(s)}\\&\lesssim  M_{\nu}\big(\lvert\!\lVert   \mathbf{R}^{+}_{s}\textcolor{red}{\mathcal{U}} -\mathbf{R}^{+}_{s} \textcolor{red}{\widetilde{\mathcal{U}}}  \rVert\!\rvert_{\gamma_1,0,O_{T+s}^s}^{(s)}\big) Q\big(
			[\  
	\mathrel{ 
		\vcenter{ 
			\hbox{ 
				\raisebox{1.5ex}{$\textcolor{red}{\bullet}$} 
				\kern-0.9ex
				\raisebox{0.6ex}{\smash{\textcolor{red}{$\vert$}}} 
			} 
		} 
	} 
 \ ]_{\overline{{O}_{T+s}^s}},
			\lvert\!\lVert  \mathbf{R}^{+}_{s}\textcolor{red}{\mathcal{U}}  \rVert\!\rvert_{\gamma_1,0,O_{T+s}^s}^{(s)},
			\lvert\!\lVert  \mathbf{R}^{+}_{s}\textcolor{red}{\widetilde{\mathcal{U}}}  \rVert\!\rvert_{\gamma_1,0,O_{T+s}^s}^{(s)}
			\big),
		\end{align*}
		where 
		\begin{align*}
			Q(b,c,d)&=\max\left\lbrace P(b,c,d),(1+b+c)(1+d)\right\rbrace\\&=\max\left\lbrace (1+b)c+1, b(1+b)^\nu(1+b+c+d),c+c^2+d,(1+b+c)(1+d)\right\rbrace.
		\end{align*}
		This concludes the proof.
